Suppose Assumptions
2.2
, and
B3.
hold.
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(-(1+a)\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{a})\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}\right],

where
e_{t}^{g}
defined in (
45
),
{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})
is defined in (
1
)
and
a>0
is a constant.

Proof.

From Lemma
B.4
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\beta_{t}{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+a){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\beta_{t}{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{a})\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(47)

Next, we will bound the first term on the RHS of (
C.2
).

We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\beta_{t}{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-2\beta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}),{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

(48)

where the inequality results from the strong convexity of
g_{t}
by Assumption
2.2
, which implies

\displaystyle\langle{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}),{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\rangle\geq\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}.

Substituting (
C.2
) into (
C.2
), gives the desired result.

∎

To simplify the notation in the analysis, we introduce the definitions

\displaystyle\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}:=\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}:=\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(49)

The following lemma, inspired by the offline bilevel optimization framework in
[
71
]
, characterizes the descent behavior of the iterates in the inner problem.

Lemma C.4
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
, and
B3.
hold.
Let
{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
be defined as in (
49
).
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
1
, the following bound is guaranteed:

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[{\theta}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]\right)

(50)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{L_{\mu_{g}}}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}[{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]+\frac{2}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\beta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{4L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}\sup_{{\bf{x}}\in\mathcal{X}}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t-1}({\bf{x}})-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|^{2}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(-\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\beta_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

where
L_{\mu_{g}}=\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
,
L_{{\bf{y}}}=\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}
is defined as in (
44
);
H_{2,T}
is defined in (
11
). Moreover,
e_{t}^{g}
is defined in (
45
).

Proof.

From Lemma
B.4
, we have for any
\acute{c}>0

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})+{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{c}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(51)

From Lemma
C.3
, we have for any
a>0

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+a\right)\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(-(1+a)\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{a}\right)\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}\right].

(52)

Substituting (
C.2
) into (
C.2
), we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\acute{c})(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(-(1+\acute{c})(1+a)\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+\acute{c})(1+\frac{1}{a})\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(53)

Choose
\acute{c}=\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}/2}{1-\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}
and
a=\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{1-2\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}
.
Let
L_{\mu_{g}}:=\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
.
Then, the following equations and inequalities are satisfied.

\displaystyle(1+\acute{c})(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2},

(54)

\displaystyle(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}},

\displaystyle(1+\acute{c})\left(1-\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2},

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a}\leq\frac{1}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}},\quad 1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\leq\frac{2}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}.

Based on (
C.2
) and (
54
), we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\left(-\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(55)

Next, we upper-bound the last term of the above inequality.

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}+\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\right),

(56)

where the second inequality is by Eq. (
39d
) in Lemma
C.1
.

Substituting (
C.2
) into (
C.2
) and summing over
t\in[T]
, give the desired result.

∎

C.3
Bounds on the Linear System Solution

Lemma C.5
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
and
B3.
hold.
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\leq(1+\acute{c})\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},

for any
\acute{c}>0
, where
{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})
is the solution of the system in Eq. (
4
).

Proof.

From the update rules in Algorithm
1
, we have the following:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|{\Pi}_{\mathcal{Z}_{p}}\big[{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}]-{\Pi}_{\mathcal{Z}_{p}}\big[{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})]\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\acute{c}){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2},

(57)

where
\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t}):=\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right){\bf{v}}_{t}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))
.

For the first term of Eq. (
C.3
) above, we
have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}-2\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\langle{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\rangle+\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}-(2\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\delta_{t}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},

(58)

where the first inequality follows from the strong convexity of the function
P_{t}
, which is the gradient of the strongly convex quadratic program
\frac{1}{2}{\bf{v}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right){\bf{v}}+{\bf{v}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))
. Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\langle{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}.

The second inequality is derived from the following inequality.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right){\bf{v}}_{t}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right)({\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},

(59)

where the second equality follows from (
4
).

Combining (
C.3
) and (
C.3
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\acute{c})\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(60)

Note that, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\leq 2(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},

which implies

\displaystyle\quad\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+2\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(61)

From (
C.3
) and (
C.3
), we get the desired result.
∎

Lemma C.6
.

Suppose Assumptions
B2.
,
B3.
,
B4.
,
C2.
and
C4.
hold.
Let
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
be generated according to Algorithm

1
.
For
e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}
defined as

\displaystyle e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}

\displaystyle:={\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}),\qquad\textnormal{where}

(62a)

\displaystyle\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})

\displaystyle:=\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}),

(62b)

we have:

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}\leq(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}(1+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}+4\lambda_{t+1}^{2}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+12p^{2}(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+72(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+144(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]+288\ell_{g,1}^{2}(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}],

(63)

for all
t\in[T]
and
(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}},\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}})
and
e_{t}^{g}
are defined in (
49
) and (
45
), respectively.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t+1}:={\bf{d}}_{t+1}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1}),

where

\displaystyle\nabla P_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1}):=\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}).

From Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf{d}}^{{\bf{v}}}_{t+1}={\bf{d}}^{{\bf{v}}{\bf{v}}}_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1}\right)+(1-\lambda_{t+1})({\bf{d}}^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}-{\bf{d}}^{{\bf{v}}{\bf{v}}}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})).

Let
{\bf{u}}=[{\bf{x}};{\bf{y}};{\bf{v}}]
.
Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+(1-\lambda_{t+1})({\bf{d}}^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1}))-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|(1-\lambda_{t+1})e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}+{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\quad-(1-\lambda_{t+1})\left({\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\right)\|^{2},

which implies that

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|\lambda_{t+1}\left({\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\right)

\displaystyle-(1-\lambda_{t+1})\left({\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}+2\lambda_{t+1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\|^{2},

where the inequality follows from Cauchy–Schwartz inequality.

For the first term, from Assumptions
C2.
and
C4.
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}}),

where the last inequality follows from (
2
).

Then, from the above inequality and
\|a+b+c\|^{2}\leq 3(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}+4\lambda_{t+1}^{2}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+6(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}.

(64)

Moreover, from
\|a+b+c\|^{2}\leq 3(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\|(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)){\bf{v}}_{t+1}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)){\bf{v}}_{t+1}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}\|^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}\right)+3\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2},

(65)

where the last inequality follows from Assumptions
B2.
,
B3.
and
B4.
;

From Eq. (
C.3
) and the inequality
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2})
, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}\right)+3\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2})

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2})

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right),

(66)

where the last inequality follows from (
C.3
).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right).

(67)

Substituting (
C.3
) and (
C.3
) into (
C.3
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}(1+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}+4\lambda_{t+1}^{2}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+6(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+72(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+144(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+288\ell_{g,1}^{2}(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}.

From
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\|a\|^{2}+2\|b\|^{2}
and (
2
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}P_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}P_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}p^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}.

This completes the proof.
∎

As demonstrated in Lemma
C.6
, the gradient estimation error
e_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}
for the linear system consists of four key components: (1) an iteratively refined error term
(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}(1+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}
, which depends on the stepsize
\delta_{t}
;
(2) the error arising from the variation in
the Hessian of the lower-level objectiv;
(3)
the error resulting from the variation in
the gradient of the upper-level objective,
and (4) approximation error terms of order
\mathcal{O}(\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}])
and
\mathcal{O}(\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}])
associated with solving the linear system and the iterates in the inner problem, respectively.

Lemma C.7
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
,
B1.
,
B2.
and
B4.
hold. Let
{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
is a solution of Subproblem (
4
). Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right),

where
\nu:=\ell_{f,1}+\frac{\ell_{g,2}\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}
.

Proof.

Based on (
4
), we have that

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)^{-1}\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))

\displaystyle-\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({{\bf{x}}_{t+1}},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)^{-1}\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\left(\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)^{-1}-\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)^{-1}\right)\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}

(68a)

\displaystyle+2\left\lVert\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)^{-1}\left(\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}.

(68b)

In the following steps, we bound the terms (
68a
) and (
68b
), respectively.

For (
68a
), we have:

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)^{-1}-\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)^{-1}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)^{-1}(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})))\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\lVert\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ell_{g,2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left\lVert\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right)-\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ell_{g,2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}+\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}\right),

(69)

where the equality holds since for any invertible matrix
{\bf{A}}
and
{\bf{B}}
we have
\|{\bf{A}}^{-1}-{\bf{B}}^{-1}\|=\|{\bf{A}}^{-1}({\bf{B}}-{\bf{A}}){\bf{B}}^{-1}\|
, and inequalities are obtained from Assumptions
2.2
and
B4.
.

Thus, from (
C.3
) and Assumption
B1.
, we get

\displaystyle\eqref{15}\leq\frac{\ell_{f,0}\ell_{g,2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\right\rVert^{2}\right).

(70)

For (
68b
), we have

(
68b
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ell_{f,1}}{\mu_{g}}\|({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ell_{f,1}}{\mu_{g}}\left(\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}\right).

(71)

Combining (
70
) and (
C.3
), we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left(\frac{\ell_{f,0}\ell_{g,2}}{\mu_{g}}+\ell_{f,1}\right)\left(\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right).

By raising both sides of the above inequality to the power 2 and using
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
, we complete the proof.
∎

The following lemma characterizes the decrease in
\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
defined in (
49
) and can be viewed as an extension of the offline BO result in
[
71
]
to the OBO setting.

Lemma C.8
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
and
2.3
hold.
Let
{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
be defined in (
49
).
Then, for any positive choice of step size
\delta_{t}
as

\delta_{t}\leq\frac{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}{\ell_{g,1}^{2}},\quad\textnormal{where}\quad\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}:=\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})},

for all
t\in[T]
,
the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
1
satisfy

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[{\theta}_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}{4}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}[{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]+\frac{8}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{16}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]

\displaystyle+\frac{16\nu^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+\frac{8\nu^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}}(1+2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2})\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2},

(72)

where
e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}
is defined in (
62
),
\nu
,
L_{{\bf{y}}}
and
{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
, are defined in Lemmas
C.7
,
C.4
and
49
, respectively.

Proof.

By Lemma
B.4
, for any
a>0
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})+{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+a\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{a}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}.

(73)

From Lemma
C.5
, we have for any
\acute{c}>0
:

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\leq(1+\acute{c})\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{v}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(74)

Substituting (
C.3
) into (
C.3
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+a\right)(1+\acute{c})\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(1+a\right)(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})(1+a)(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{a}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}.

(75)

In the following, we provide a bound for the third term on the right-hand side of (
C.3
).
To this end, we have from Lemma
C.7
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(2{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left((1+2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right),

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
C.1
.

Combining this result with (
C.3
) gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+a\right)(1+\acute{c})\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(1+a\right)(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+4(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})(1+a)(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}})\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]

\displaystyle+4\left(1+\frac{1}{a}\right)\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left(1+\frac{1}{a}\right)\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}(1+2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}.

(76)

Let
\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}=\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
, then we have

\displaystyle 1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}

\displaystyle=1-2\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}},

(77)

where the last inequality follows from
\delta_{t}\leq\frac{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}{\ell_{g,1}^{2}}
.

Choose
a=\frac{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}/4}{1-\frac{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}{2}}
and
\acute{c}=\frac{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}/2}{1-\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}
.
Then, from (
C.3
), we have

\displaystyle\left(1+a\right)(1+\acute{c})\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{(\ell_{g,1}+\ell_{g,1}^{3})\mu_{g}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\delta_{t}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\right)

(78)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+a\right)\left(1+\acute{c}\right)\left(1-\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\frac{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}{4},

\displaystyle(1+a)\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\right)\leq\frac{4}{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}},

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\leq\frac{2}{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}},\quad 1+\frac{1}{a}\leq\frac{4}{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}.

Thus, from (
C.3
) and (
78
) we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\delta_{t}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}{4}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{8}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{16}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}(p^{2}\ell_{g,2}^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]

\displaystyle+\frac{16\nu^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}\delta_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{y}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+\frac{8\nu^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}\delta_{t}}(1+2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}.

Rearranging the terms and summing from
t=1
to
T
, gives the desired result.
∎

C.4
Bounds on the Gradient Estimation Error of Outer Objective

The following lemma, inspired by
[
71
]
, provides a characterization of the descent of the gradient estimation error for the outer-level function.

Lemma C.9
.

Suppose Assumptions
B2.
,
B3.
,
B4.
,
C3.
and
C5.
hold.
Let
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
be generated according to Algorithm

1
.
For
e_{t}^{f}
defined as

\displaystyle e^{f}_{t}:={{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right),\quad\textnormal{where}\quad\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)=\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t},

(79)

we have:

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+4\eta_{t+1}^{2}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+12p^{2}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+72(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+72(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}],

(80)

for all
t\in[T]
,
\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
,
e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}
and
e_{t}^{g}
are defined in (
49
), (
62
) and (
45
), respectively.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle e^{f}_{t+1}={{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{\bf{x}}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1}\right),

where

\displaystyle\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1}\right)=\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})+\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}.

(81)

From Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf{d}}^{{\bf{x}}}_{t+1}={\bf{d}}^{{\bf{x}}{\bf{x}}}_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1}\right)+(1-\eta_{t+1})({\bf{d}}^{{\bf{x}}}_{t+1}-{\bf{d}}^{{\bf{x}}{\bf{x}}}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})),

where
{\bf{d}}^{{\bf{xx}}}_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1}\right)=\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}
.

Let
{\bf{u}}=[{\bf{x}};{\bf{y}};{\bf{v}}]
.
Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{\bf{x}}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}\left({\bf{u}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+(1-\eta_{t+1})({\bf{d}}^{{\bf{x}}}_{t}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1}))-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|(1-\eta_{t+1})e_{t}^{f}+\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\quad-(1-\eta_{t+1})(\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t}))\|^{2},

which implies that

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|\eta_{t+1}(\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle-(1-\eta_{t+1})(\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+2\eta_{t+1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\|^{2},

(82)

where the inequality follows from
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\|a\|^{2}+2\|b\|^{2}
.

Let us bound the second term in the right-hand side of (
C.4
).
Based on (
81
), we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}}),

where the first inequality is by and
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\|a\|^{2}+2\|b\|^{2}
; the second inequality follows from Assumptions
C3.
,
C5.
and (
2
).

Substituting the above inequality into (
C.4
) and using
\|a+b+c\|^{2}\leq 3(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2})
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+4\lambda_{t+1}^{2}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+6(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}.

(83)

Moreover, from
\|a+b+c\|^{2}\leq 3(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}+\|c\|^{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}3{\mathbb{E}}\|(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)){\bf{v}}_{t+1}+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)){\bf{v}}_{t+1}+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}\|^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}\right)+3\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(iii)}{\leq}6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}\right)+3\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{d}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(iv)}{\leq}6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2})

\displaystyle\overset{(vi)}{\leq}6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right),

(84)

where the (i) follows from (
81
); (ii) follows from Assumptions
B2.
,
B3.
and
B4.
;
(iii) follows from (
2
); (iv) follows from (
45
) and (
62
); (vi) follows from (
C.3
).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right).

(85)

Substituting (
C.4
) and (
C.4
) into (
C.4
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+4\eta_{t+1}^{2}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+6(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+72(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+72(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}.

From
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\|a\|^{2}+2\|b\|^{2}
and (
2
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}({\bf{u}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\right){\bf{v}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}p^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}.

This completes the proof.
∎

As demonstrated in Lemma
C.9
, the hypergradient estimator error
e_{t+1}^{f}
comprises five key components: (1)
the term
(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}
, representing the per-iteration improvement achieved by the momentum-based update;
(2) the error arising from the variation in the Jacobian
of the lower-level objectiv; (3) the error caused by the variation in the gradient of the
upper-level objective
; (4) the error
term
\mathcal{O}(2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2})
, which is due to
solving the lower-level problem; and (5) the error term
\mathcal{O}(\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+72(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}])
, which is introduced by the
one-step momentum update in solving the linear system problem.

C.5
Bounds on the Outer Objective and its Projected Gradient

Lemma C.10
.

Let Assumption
2.4
holds.
Then, for the sequence of functions
\{f_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)\leq 2M+V_{T},

where
M
is defined in Assumption
2.4
;
V_{T}
is defined in (
11
).

Proof.

Note that, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle=f_{1}({\bf{x}}_{1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{1}({\bf{x}}_{1}))-f_{T}({\bf{x}}_{T+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{T}({\bf{x}}_{T+1}))

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t-1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t-1}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2M+V_{T},

where the inequality follows from Assumption
2.4
.
∎

Lemma C.11
.

Let
\{f_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
denote the sequence of functions presented to Algorithm
1
, satisfying Assumptions
2.2
,
2.3
and
2.4
.
Let
\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}
be defined as in Definition
B.1
. For any positive step size
\alpha_{t}
such that
\alpha_{t}\leq{1}/4{L_{f}}
for all
t\in[T]
, Algorithm
1
ensures the following bound:

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8M+4V_{T}+2M_{f}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)\left({\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]+{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right)

\displaystyle+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}.

(86)

Here,
\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
and
\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
are defined in (
49
);

V_{T}
,
M
,
M_{f}
and
e^{f}_{t}
are defined in (
11
), Assumption
2.4
, Eq. (
42
), and (
79
).

Proof.

It follows from Lemma
C.1
that

\displaystyle\quad f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rangle+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=-\alpha_{t}\left\langle\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle+\frac{L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}}{2}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}.

(87)

For the first term on the right hand side of (
C.5
), we have that

\displaystyle\quad-\left\langle\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\left\langle{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}},\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle-\left\langle\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}},\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2},

where the inequality follows from Lemma
B.7
.

Let
\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)=\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}
. Then, from Lemma
C.1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)+\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right),

(88)

where
e^{f}_{t}={{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\tilde{{\bf{d}}}_{t}\left({\bf{z}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)
. This implies that

\displaystyle\quad-\left\langle\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right).

(89)

Plugging the bound (
C.5
) into (
C.5
), we have that

\displaystyle\quad f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}-\alpha_{t})}{2}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}+2\alpha_{t}\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)\alpha_{t},

which can be rearranged into

\displaystyle\quad(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2})\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))+4\alpha_{t}\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+2M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)\alpha_{t}.

(90)

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+4M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)+4\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2},

(91)

where the second inequaliy follows from non-expansiveness of the projection
operator and the last inequality follows from (
C.5
).

Combining (
C.5
) and (
C.5
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle+2M_{f}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8M+4V_{T}

\displaystyle+2M_{f}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)+2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)\left\lVert e^{f}_{t}\right\rVert^{2},

where the second inequality is due to Lemma
C.10
.
∎

Lemma C.12
.

Let Assumptions
2.2
, and
2.3
hold.
Let
\{{\bf{x}}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
be generated according to Algorithm

1
.
Then, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}+M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)\right),

where
\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
and
\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
are defined in (
49
),
M_{f}
is defined in (
42
).

Proof.

From the update rule of Algorithm
1
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{t}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left\lVert{\bf{d}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}+M_{f}^{2}\left(\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right)\right),

(92)

where the first inequality is by
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
; the second inequality follows from non-expansiveness of the projection
operator; and the last inequality follows from Eq. (
39a
) in Lemma
C.1
.
∎

C.6
Proof of Theorem
2.6

Proof.

Bounding
{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}
in (
C.9
)
.
From (
C.9
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}\leq\left(\frac{(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+\frac{4\eta_{t+1}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}})

\displaystyle+\frac{12p^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{12}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{72}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{72}{\alpha_{t}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{72}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}].

(93)

With respect to the coefficient of the first term on the right-hand side of Eq. (
C.6
), it is important to note that we have:

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}.

(94)

Using the definition of
\alpha_{t}
in (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=(c+t)^{1/3}-(c+t-1)^{1/3}\overset{(i)}{\leq}\frac{1}{3(c+t-1)^{2/3}}\overset{(ii)}{\leq}\frac{1}{3(\frac{c}{2}+t)^{2/3}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{2/3}}{3(c+2t)^{2/3}}\overset{(iii)}{\leq}\frac{2^{2/3}}{3(c+t)^{2/3}}\overset{(iv)}{\leq}\frac{2^{2/3}}{3}\alpha_{t}^{2}\overset{(vi)}{\leq}\frac{\alpha_{t}}{6L_{f}},

(95)

where the (i) follows from
(a+b)^{1/3}-a^{1/3}\leq b/(3a^{2/3})
; (ii) follows from
c\geq 2
in (
109
); (iii) follows from (
2.6
); (iv) follows from
\alpha_{t}\leq 1/4L_{f}
in (
109
).

Substituting (
C.6
) into (
94
) and using
\delta_{t}=c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}
and
\eta_{t+1}=c_{\eta}\alpha_{t}^{2}
in Eq. (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}}{6L_{f}}-\frac{\eta_{t+1}}{\alpha_{t}}=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{6L_{f}}-c_{\eta}\alpha_{t}\leq-5\Omega\alpha_{t},

(96)

where the inequalities follow from
c_{\eta}=\frac{1}{6L_{f}}+5\Omega
in (
109
).

Then, substituting (
96
) into (
C.6
) yields

\displaystyle\quad\frac{1}{\Omega}{\mathbb{E}}\left(\frac{\|e_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}\right)\leq-5\alpha_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{f}\|^{2}+\frac{4\eta_{t+1}^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{b}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{\acute{b}})

\displaystyle+\frac{12p^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{12}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{72}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{72}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e^{{\bf{v}}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{72}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{4}\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}].

(97)

Bounding
{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}
in (
C.2
)
.

From (
C.2
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{g}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}(1+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}\beta_{t}^{2})-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\gamma_{t+1}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{b}+\frac{24}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{6}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+48(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\beta_{t}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(98)

Let us examine the coefficient of the first term on the right-hand side of Eq. (
C.6
).
Specifically, for
\gamma_{t+1}=c_{\gamma}\alpha_{t}^{2}
and
\beta_{t}=c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}
in Eq. (
2.6
), we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}(1+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}\beta_{t}^{2})-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})(1+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}\beta_{t}^{2})-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}-\frac{\gamma_{t+1}}{\alpha_{t}}+\frac{1-\gamma_{t+1}}{\alpha_{t}}48\ell_{g,1}^{2}\beta_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}-c_{\gamma}\alpha_{t}+(\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}-c_{\gamma}\alpha_{t})48\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\alpha_{t}}{6L_{f}}+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}\alpha_{t}-c_{\gamma}\alpha_{t},

(99)

where the last inequality follows from (
C.6
).

From the selected
c_{\gamma}
in (
109
) and the definition of
\Phi
in (
C.6
), we have

\displaystyle c_{\gamma}=\frac{1}{6L_{f}}+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}+\hbar\Phi,\quad\textnormal{where}\quad\hbar=25\frac{M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}.

Combined this with Eq. (
C.6
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}(1+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}\beta_{t}^{2})-\frac{1}{\alpha_{t-1}}

\displaystyle\leq-\hbar\Phi\alpha_{t}.

(100)

Substituting Eq. (
100
) into Eq. (
C.6
) yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Phi}\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{g}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq-\hbar\alpha_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\gamma_{t+1}^{2}}{\Phi\alpha_{t}}\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{b}+\frac{24}{\Phi\alpha_{t}}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}
