\displaystyle+144(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\frac{\beta_{t}^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{22M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}\alpha_{t}-\hbar\alpha_{t}+144(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})(\frac{1}{\Psi}+\frac{1}{\Omega})c_{\beta}^{2}\alpha_{t}

\displaystyle\leq 24\frac{M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}\alpha_{t}-\hbar\alpha_{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}\alpha_{t},

where the second inequality follows from

\Omega,\Psi\geq 144(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\frac{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}}{M_{f}^{2}}
in (
C.6
); and the last equality is by
\hbar=25\frac{M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}
.

From
\delta_{t}=c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}
in (
2.6
), we obtain

\displaystyle I(\alpha_{t},\delta_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{4\Upsilon}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}\delta_{t}-\jmath\alpha_{t}+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}\frac{\delta_{t}^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\Upsilon}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}}c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}-\jmath\alpha_{t}+72\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{c_{\delta}^{2}\alpha_{t}}{\Omega}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{89M_{f}^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}\alpha_{t}-\jmath\alpha_{t}

\displaystyle=-\frac{M_{f}^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}\alpha_{t},

where the second inequality follows from
\Upsilon=\frac{22M_{f}^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}c_{\delta}}
and
\Omega\geq\frac{72\ell_{g,1}^{2}\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}{M_{f}^{2}}c_{\delta}^{2}
in (
C.6
); the last equality follows from
\jmath=90\frac{M_{f}^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}
.

Thus, we get

\displaystyle\eqref{A12}\leq 0.

(118)

Bounding (
110c
)
.

From
\beta_{t}=c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}
in (
2.6
) and Eq. (
112
), we have

\displaystyle L(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t})

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\Gamma\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\Gamma\beta_{t}^{2}+48(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\beta_{t}^{2}}{\Phi\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle+144(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\frac{\beta_{t}^{2}}{\Psi\alpha_{t}}+144(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\frac{\beta_{t}^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{2\Gamma c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\Gamma c_{\beta}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}+48\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\Phi}+144(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})(\frac{1}{\Psi}+\frac{1}{\Omega})c_{\beta}^{2}\alpha_{t}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{2\Gamma c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\Gamma c_{\beta}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}+\frac{3\Gamma c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}}{4(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\Gamma c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}}{4(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})},

where the second inequality is by
\Phi\geq 192\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})}{\Gamma}c_{\beta}
, and
\Omega,\Psi\geq 576(\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{2}+\ell_{f,1}^{2})\frac{(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})}{\Gamma}c_{\beta}
in (
C.6
); the last inequality follows from
\alpha_{t}\leq{1}/{c_{\beta}(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})}
in (
109
).

From
\beta_{t}=c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}
,
\delta_{t}=c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}
in (
2.6
) and Eq. (
112
), we obtain

\displaystyle N(\beta_{t},\delta_{t})

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{L_{\mu_{g}}\beta_{t}}\Gamma+\frac{16\nu^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}\delta_{t}}\Upsilon=\frac{4}{L_{\mu_{g}}c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}}\Gamma+\frac{16\nu^{2}}{\acute{L}_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}}\Upsilon.

Thus, we get

(
110c
)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}L(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t}){\mathbb{E}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+\sum_{t=2}^{T}N(\beta_{t},\delta_{t})\sup_{{\bf{x}}\in\mathcal{X}}\|{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t-1}({\bf{x}})-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{H_{2,T}}{\alpha_{T}}\right).

(119)

Bounding (
110d
)
.

From
\eta_{t+1}=c_{\eta}\alpha_{t}^{2}
,
\gamma_{t+1}=c_{\gamma}\alpha_{t}^{2}
,
\lambda_{t+1}=c_{\lambda}\alpha_{t}^{2}
in Eq. (
2.6
), we obtain

(
110d
)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}\frac{2}{\Phi}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\gamma_{t+1}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}+\frac{4}{\Psi}\left(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\lambda_{t+1}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}+\frac{4}{\Omega}\left(\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}p^{2}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\eta_{t+1}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left((\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}+\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{{b}}+\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}+\frac{\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}\right).

(120)

Bounding (
110e
)
.

We also have

(
110e
)

\displaystyle=\frac{6}{\Phi\alpha_{T}}G_{{\bf{y}},T}+\frac{12p^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{T}}G_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}},T}+\frac{12p^{2}}{\Psi\alpha_{T}}G_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}},T}+\frac{12\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{\Psi\alpha_{T}}D_{{\bf{y}},T}+\frac{12\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{T}}D_{{\bf{x}},T}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}}(G_{{\bf{y}},T}+G_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}},T}+G_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}},T}+D_{{\bf{y}},T}+D_{{\bf{x}},T})\right).

(121)

From Eq. (
2.6
), we have
b=\bar{b}=1
. Moreover, by (
10
),
\sigma^{2}=\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}+\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}+\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}+\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}+\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}
. From (
15
), we also have

\displaystyle G_{T}=G_{{\bf{y}},T}+G_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}},T}+G_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}},T},

\displaystyle D_{T}=D_{{\bf{y}},T}+D_{{\bf{x}},T}.

Then, by inequalities (
C.6
), (
117
), (
118
), (
C.6
), (
C.6
), (
C.6
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}+\Lambda

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(V_{T}+\frac{H_{2,T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}+\frac{G_{T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{D_{T}}{\alpha_{T}}\right).

(122)

From the definition of
\Lambda
in (
C.6
), we have

\displaystyle-\Lambda

\displaystyle=\Gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}]\right)+\Upsilon\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right)+\frac{1}{\Phi}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{g}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\Psi}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)+\frac{1}{\Omega}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{f}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t-1}}-\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t+1}^{f}\|^{2}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma\theta_{1}^{{\bf{y}}}+\Upsilon\theta_{1}^{{\bf{v}}}+\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\Phi\alpha_{0}}+\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}+\sigma_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\Psi\alpha_{0}}+\frac{\sigma_{g_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}}^{2}+\sigma_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{\Omega\alpha_{0}}.

(123)

Using (
C.6
), we get

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(V_{T}+\frac{H_{2,T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}+\frac{G_{T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{D_{T}}{\alpha_{T}}-\Lambda\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(V_{T}+\theta_{1}^{{\bf{y}}}+\theta_{1}^{{\bf{v}}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{b}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}+\frac{H_{2,T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{G_{T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{D_{T}}{\alpha_{T}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha_{0}}\right).

Since
\alpha_{t}={1}/{(c+t)^{1/3}}
in Eq. (
2.6
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{3}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{c+t}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{1+t}\leq\log(T+1),

which, combined with the fact that
\alpha_{t}
decreases with respect to
t
and by multiplying both sides by
{2}/{\alpha_{T}}
, results in
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\textnormal{BL-Reg}_{T}=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}}(V_{T}+\|{\bf{y}}_{1}-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{1}({\bf{x}}_{1})\|^{2}+\|{\bf{v}}_{1}-{\bf{v}}^{*}_{1}({\bf{x}}_{1})\|^{2}+\sigma^{2}\log(T+1)+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\alpha_{0}})

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}^{2}}(H_{2,T}+G_{T}+D_{T})\Big).

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Proof of Regret Bounds for Zeroth Order SOGD (ZO-SOGD)

Proof Roadmap
.
We provide Lemma
D.7
, which quantifies the error between the approximated direction of the
momentum-based gradient estimator,
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
and the true direction,
\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})
, at each iteration.
Lemma
D.9
assesses the convergence of the iterative solutions
\{{\bf{y}}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
, specifically the gap
{\mathbb{E}}\big[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\big]
, while accounting for the error introduced in Lemma
D.7
.
To establish Lemma
D.13
, which quantifies the error between the approximated direction of the
momentum-based gradient estimator,
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
, and the true direction,
\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}
, we first present Lemma
D.11
. This lemma quantifies the error between
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
and
{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}))
.
Then, Lemma
D.15
captures the error of the system solution to Problem (
18
), i.e., the gap
{\mathbb{E}}\big[\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\big]
, based on these errors.
To establish Lemma
D.19
, which quantifies the error between
the approximated direction of
the momentum-based hypergradient estimator,

\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}
, and the
true direction,
\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}
, we introduce Lemma
D.17
. This lemma quantifies the error between
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}
and
\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}))
. Finally,
Lemma
D.20
bounds the projection mapping based on these errors. By combining these lemmas and properly setting the parameters, we achieve the desired result.

D.1
Auxiliary Lemmas for Proof of Theorem
3.2

Lemma D.1
.

[
4
, Lemma A.1.]

Suppose Assumption
B4.
holds. Then, for any
{\bf{x}},{\bf{v}}\in\mathcal{X}
, we have:

\left\lVert\nabla g_{t}({\bf{x}}+{\bf{v}},{\bf{y}}+{\bf{v}})-\nabla g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla^{2}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}){\bf{v}}\right\rVert\leq\ell_{g,2}\left\lVert{\bf{v}}\right\rVert^{2}.

Lemma D.2
.

Suppose that Assumptions
2.2
and
2.3
hold for all
{\bf{x}},{\bf{x}}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}
, and
t\in[T]
, and that
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{x}}}
and
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}
are defined in (
22
). Then, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))\|^{2}\leq M_{f}^{2}\left(\left\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\|^{2}\right),

(124a)

\displaystyle\left\|{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}\right\|^{2}\leq M_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}\left(\left\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\|^{2}+\left\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\|^{2}\right),

(124b)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}^{\prime},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}^{\prime}))\right\|\leq L_{f}\left\|{\bf{x}}-{\bf{x}}^{\prime}\right\|,

(124c)

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}^{\prime})\right\|\leq L_{{\bf{y}}}\left\|{\bf{x}}-{\bf{x}}^{\prime}\right\|,

(124d)

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}^{\prime})\right\|\leq L_{{\bf{v}}}\left\|{\bf{x}}-{\bf{x}}^{\prime}\right\|.

(124e)

Here,
\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
,
f_{t,\bm{\rho}}
and
\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
are defined in (
20
), (
18
), and (
19
), respectively. Moreover, the constants
M_{f}
,
M_{{\bf{v}}}
, and
(L_{{\bf{y}}},L_{{\bf{v}}},L_{f})
are defined as in (
42
), (
43
), and (
44
), respectively.

Proof.

We first show Eq. (
124a
).

Using Assumptions
2.2
and
B1.
, we have
\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\right)\succeq\mu_{g}
, and

\displaystyle\|\hat{{\bf{v}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\|=\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\right)\right)^{-1}\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\right)\|\leq\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}.

(125)

Observe that we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}{f}_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}{f}_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}))\|

\displaystyle+\|{\bf{v}}\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\right)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}{f}_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}{f}_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}))\|

\displaystyle+\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\|\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|

\displaystyle+\|\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}))\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\ell_{f,1}+\frac{{\ell}_{g,2}\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}\right)\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\|+\ell_{g,1}\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|

\displaystyle\leq M_{f}^{2}\left(\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\|+\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|\right),

(126)

where
M_{f}
is defined as in (
42
);
the third inequality is by Assumption
2.3
and the last inequality is by Eq. (
125
).

We now show Eq. (
124b
).

Since
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}*}:=\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}))+\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\right)\hat{{\bf{v}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})=0
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}*}\|

\displaystyle=\|{\bf{v}}_{t}\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})

\displaystyle-\left(\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\right)+\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))\right)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\left(\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}))\right)\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|

\displaystyle+\|\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\left({\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right)\|

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}))\|.

Then, from Assumption
2.3
and Eq. (
125
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}\|

\displaystyle\leq{\ell}_{g,2}\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|\|\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|+\ell_{g,1}\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|+\ell_{f,1}\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{{\ell}_{g,2}\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}+\ell_{f,1}\right)\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|+\ell_{g,1}\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|

\displaystyle\leq M_{{\bf{v}}}\left(\|{\bf{y}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}})\|+\|{\bf{v}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|\right),

where
M_{{\bf{v}}}

is defined as in (
43
).

The proofs of Eqs. (
124c
)-(
124e
) follow from
[
69
, Lemma 17]
by setting
(L_{{\bf{y}}},L_{{\bf{v}}},L_{f})
as in (
44
).
∎

D.2
Perturbation Bounds for OBO Objectives and Their Smoothing Variants

The following two lemmas are inspired by
[
25
]
.

Lemma D.3
.

Given
\bm{\rho}=(\rho_{{\bf{s}}},\rho_{\mathbf{r}})
as positive smoothing parameters, let
g_{t,\bm{\rho}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
and
f_{t,\bm{\rho}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
be the functions defined by (
18
).

(a)

Suppose Assumption
B3.
holds. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left|g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right|\leq\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{2}.

(127)

(b)

Suppose Assumption
B2.
holds. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left|f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-f_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right|\leq\frac{\ell_{f,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{2}.

(128)

Proof.

Let
B_{1}
and
B_{2}
be the unit
ball in
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}}
and
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}}
, respectively. Let
\mathcal{V}(d_{1})
and
\mathcal{V}(d_{2})
be volume of the unit ball in
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}}
and
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}}
, respectively. Then, we have

\displaystyle\quad\left|g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\mathcal{V}(d_{1})\mathcal{V}(d_{2})}\int_{{B_{1}}}\int_{{B_{2}}}\left(g_{t}({\bf{x}}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right)d{\bf{s}}d{\bf{r}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{1}{\mathcal{V}(d_{1})\mathcal{V}(d_{2})}\int_{{B_{1}}}\int_{{B_{2}}}\left(g_{t}({\bf{x}}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\left\langle\nabla g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}})\right\rangle\right)d{\bf{s}}d{\bf{r}}\right|.

Thus, we get

\displaystyle\quad\left|g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{{B_{1}}}\int_{{B_{2}}}\left|g_{t}({\bf{x}}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\langle\nabla g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}})\rangle\right|d{\bf{s}}d{\bf{r}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{{B_{1}}}\int_{{B_{2}}}\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{2}\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}\|{\bf{s}}\|^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\|{\bf{r}}\|^{2}\right)d{\bf{s}}d{\bf{r}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell_{g,1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{2}\int_{{B_{1}}}\|{\bf{s}}\|^{2}d{\bf{s}}+\frac{\ell_{g,1}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{2}\int_{{B_{2}}}\|{\bf{r}}\|^{2}d{\bf{r}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\ell_{g,1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{2}\frac{d_{1}}{d_{1}+2}+\frac{\ell_{g,1}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{2}\frac{d_{2}}{d_{2}+2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{2},

where the last equality follows since
\frac{1}{\mathcal{V}(d)}\int_{s\in B}\|s\|^{p}ds=\frac{d}{d+p}
.

The proof of part (b) follows using similar arguments.
∎

Lemma D.4
.

Given
\bm{\rho}=(\rho_{{\bf{s}}},\rho_{\mathbf{r}})
as positive smoothing parameters, let
g_{t,\bm{\rho}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
and
f_{t,\bm{\rho}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
be the functions defined by (
18
).

(a)

Suppose Assumption
B3.
holds. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert\leq\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}d_{1}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}d_{2})}{2}.

(129)

(b)

Suppose Assumption
B2.
holds. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla f_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert\leq\frac{\ell_{f,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}d_{1}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}d_{2})}{2}.

(130)

Proof.

Let
S(d_{1})
be the surface area of the
unit sphere in
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}}
. Moreover, let
U_{B_{1}}
be the unit sphere.

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\frac{1}{S(d_{1})}\left(\frac{d_{1}}{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}}\int_{U_{B_{1}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}}){\bf{s}}d{\bf{s}}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{S(d_{1})}\left(\frac{d_{1}}{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}}\int_{U_{B_{1}}}{g_{t}({\bf{x}}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}}){\bf{s}}d{\bf{s}}}-\int_{U_{B_{1}}}{\frac{d_{1}}{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}){\bf{s}}d{\bf{s}}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\int_{U_{B_{1}}}{\frac{d_{1}}{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}}\langle\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}}\rangle}{\bf{s}}d{\bf{s}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{1}}{S(d_{1})\rho_{{\bf{s}}}}\int_{U_{B_{1}}}\Big|g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\langle\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}}\rangle\Big|\left\lVert{\bf{s}}\right\rVert d{\bf{s}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{1}}{S(d_{1})\rho_{{\bf{s}}}}\cdot\frac{\ell_{g,1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{2}\int_{U_{B_{1}}}\left\lVert{\bf{s}}\right\rVert^{3}d{\bf{s}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}}{2},

(131)

where the second equality follows from
\int_{U_{B_{1}}}{\bf{s}}{\bf{s}}^{\top}d{\bf{s}}=\frac{S(d_{1})}{d_{1}}{\bf{I}}
.

Similarly, let
S(d_{2})
be the surface area of the
unit sphere in
{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}}
. Moreover, let
U_{B_{2}}
be the unit sphere.

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\frac{1}{S(d_{2})}\left(\frac{d_{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}}\int_{U_{B_{2}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}}){\bf{r}}d{\bf{r}}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{S(d_{2})}\left(\frac{d_{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}}\int_{U_{B_{2}}}{g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}}){\bf{r}}d{\bf{r}}}-\int_{U_{B_{2}}}{\frac{d_{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}){\bf{r}}d{\bf{r}}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\int_{U_{B_{2}}}{\frac{d_{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}}\langle\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}}\rangle}{\bf{r}}d{\bf{r}}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{2}}{S(d_{2})\rho_{{\bf{r}}}}\int_{U_{B_{2}}}\big|g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}})-g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\langle\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}}\rangle\big|\left\lVert{\bf{r}}\right\rVert d{\bf{r}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{2}}{S(d_{2})\rho_{{\bf{r}}}}\cdot\frac{\ell_{g,1}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{2}\int_{U_{B_{2}}}\left\lVert{\bf{r}}\right\rVert^{3}d{\bf{r}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}}{2},

(132)

where the second equality follows from
\int_{U_{B_{2}}}{\bf{r}}{\bf{r}}^{\top}d{\bf{r}}=\frac{S(d_{2})}{d_{2}}{\bf{I}}
.

Thus, we get

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert{\nabla}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert+\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\right\rVert

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}}{2}+\frac{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}}{2}.

Finally, by a similar argument as in Part (a), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left(\mathbf{x},{\bf{y}}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}\left(\mathbf{x},{\bf{y}}\right)\right\rVert\leq\frac{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}d_{1}\ell_{f,1}}{2},

(133)

and

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left(\mathbf{x},{\bf{y}}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}\left(\mathbf{x},{\bf{y}}\right)\right\rVert\leq\frac{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}d_{2}\ell_{f,1}}{2},

(134)

which implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left(\mathbf{x},{\bf{y}}\right)-\nabla f_{t}\left(\mathbf{x},{\bf{y}}\right)\right\rVert\leq\frac{(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}d_{1}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}d_{2})\ell_{f,1}}{2}.

∎

Lemma D.5
.

Suppose Assumption
B3.
holds.
Let
\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})
and
\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})
be defined as in (
24a
) and (
24b
), respectively. Then, for any
({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}}\times{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}}
and
\rho_{{\bf{r}}},\rho_{{\bf{s}}}\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\underset{({\bf{r}},\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({{\bf{x}}},\acute{{\bf{y}}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\leq 3d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}-\acute{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{2},

(135a)

\displaystyle\underset{({\bf{s}},\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}(\acute{{\bf{x}}},{{\bf{y}}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\leq 3d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{x}}-\acute{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{2},

(135b)

for all
\acute{{\bf{y}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{2}}
and
\acute{{\bf{x}}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}}
.

Proof.

The proof is similar to that of Lemma 5 in
[
43
]
.
∎

Lemma D.6
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
and
B3.
hold. Let
(\rho_{{\bf{s}}},\rho_{\mathbf{r}})
be positive smoothing parameters. Let
{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
and
\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
be defined in (
1
) and (
19
), respectively. Then, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}\right]\leq\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}.

(136)

Proof.

From (
1
), we have
\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\in\arg\min_{\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}}g_{t}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
. Since, by Assumption
2.2
,
g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}\right)
is
\mu_{g}
-strongly
convex with respect to
{\bf{y}}
, it follows from Lemma
B.2
that

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bf{y}}-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{g}}\left(g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}\right)-g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right).

By setting
{\bf{y}}=\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{g}}\left(g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)-g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right).

(137)

Similarly, from (
19
), we have

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\in\arg\min_{\mathbf{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}}\left\{g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}})=\underset{(\mathbf{s},\mathbf{r},\zeta_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[g_{t}({\bf{x}}+\rho_{{\bf{s}}}{\bf{s}},{\bf{y}}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}{\bf{r}};\zeta_{t})\right]\right\},

where
\bm{\rho}=(\rho_{{\bf{s}}},\rho_{{\bf{r}}})
.
By Assumption
2.2
,
g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}\right)
is
\mu_{g}
-strongly
convex with respect to
{\bf{y}}
.
Hence, according to Lemma
B.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\lVert{\bf{y}}-\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{g}}\left(g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}\right)-g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right).

By setting
{\bf{y}}=\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})
, we have

\displaystyle\left\lVert{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{g}}\left(g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}))-g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))\right).

(138)

Summing up (
137
) and (
138
), we get

\displaystyle\left\lVert{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left(g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}))-g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left(g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)-g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))\right),

which implies

\displaystyle\left\lVert{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left|g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}))-g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\mathbf{y}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{g}}\left|g_{t}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x})\right)-g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}},

where the last inequality is by Eq. (
127
).
∎

D.3
Bounds on the Zeroth-Order Inner Solution

Recall that
{\bf{s}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}
and
{\bf{r}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}
are vectors uniformly sampled from the unit balls
B_{1}
and
B_{2}
, respectively.
Let

\displaystyle{U}_{{b}}^{\bf{s}}=\{{\bf{s}}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}\}_{i=1}^{b},\quad{U}_{b}^{\bf{r}}=\{{\bf{r}}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}\}_{i=1}^{b},

\displaystyle{U}_{\bar{b}}^{\bf{s}}=\{{\bf{s}}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{1}}\}_{i=1}^{\bar{b}},\quad{U}_{\bar{b}}^{\bf{r}}=\{{\bf{r}}_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{2}}\}_{i=1}^{\bar{b}},

be generated from the uniform distributions over the unit spheres
(U_{B_{1}},U_{B_{2}})
. Here,
(U_{B_{1}},U_{B_{2}})
denote the uniform distributions over the
(d_{1},d_{2})
-dimensional unit Euclidean balls
(B_{1},B_{2})
, respectively.

Then, similar to (
3
), we have

\displaystyle\underset{({U}_{{b}}^{\bf{r}},{\mathcal{B}}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\mathcal{B}_{t})\right]={\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),\quad\underset{({U}_{{b}}^{\bf{s}},{\mathcal{B}}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\mathcal{B}_{t})\right]={\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),

\displaystyle\underset{({U}_{{b}}^{\bf{r}},\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})\right]={\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}),\quad\underset{({U}_{{b}}^{\bf{s}},\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t})\right]={\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},{\bf{y}}).

(139)

Lemma D.7
.

Suppose that Assumptions
B3.
and
D1.
hold.
Consider the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm

2
, and define

\displaystyle e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}:=\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}.

(140)

Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}+12(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t-1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+9d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+24d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+24d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}\gamma_{t+1}^{2}.

(141)

Proof.

From the definition of
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}
in Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}

\displaystyle=-\gamma_{t+1}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\gamma_{t+1}\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle+(1-\gamma_{t+1})\left(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-(\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}+\gamma_{t+1}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-\gamma_{t+1}\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle-(1-\gamma_{t+1})\left(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|(1-\gamma_{t+1})(\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}})

\displaystyle+\gamma_{t+1}(\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle+(1-\gamma_{t+1})\left(\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right)\|^{2}.

From (
D.3
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right]=\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right]

\displaystyle=\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}),

then, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}\|\gamma_{t+1}(\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle+(1-\gamma_{t+1})\left(\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})

\displaystyle-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\gamma_{t+1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2},

where the second inequality holds by
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

Then, from
{\mathbb{E}}\|a-{\mathbb{E}}[a]\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}\|a\|^{2}-\|{\mathbb{E}}[a]\|^{2}
and Assumption
D1.
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+4(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+4(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}+2\gamma_{t+1}^{2}\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+4(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+2\gamma_{t+1}^{2}\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}},

where the second inequality follows from Young’s inequality and Lemma
D.5
.

From Eq. (
D.2
), we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2}.

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}\leq(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+6(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+3(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+2\gamma_{t+1}^{2}\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}.

∎

Lemma D.8
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
and
B3.
hold.
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(-(1+a)\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{a})\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}\right],

where
a>0
is a constant,
e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}
is defined in (
140
), and

\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})
is defined in (
19
).

Proof.

From Lemma
B.4
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\beta_{t}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+a){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\beta_{t}{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{a})\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(142)

Next, we will separately bound the first term on the RHS of the above inequality.

We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\beta_{t}{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-2\beta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}),{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

(143)

where the inequality results from the strong convexity of
g_{t,\bm{\rho}}
by Assumption
2.2
, which implies

\displaystyle\langle{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}),{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\rangle\geq\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}.

Substituting (
D.3
) into (
D.3
), gives the desired result.

∎

For notational brevity in the analysis, we define

\displaystyle\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}:=\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},\quad\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}:=\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2},

(144)

where
\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
and
\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})
are defined in (
19
) and (
20
), respectively.

Lemma D.9
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
and
B3.
hold.
Let
\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
be defined in (
144
).
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
2
guarantees the following bound:

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(-\frac{L_{\mu_{g}}}{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]+\frac{2}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}\right]\right)\beta_{t}+\frac{4L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{24\ell_{g,1}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})+\frac{12}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sup_{{\bf{x}}\in\mathcal{X}}\|{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t-1}({\bf{x}})-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\beta_{t}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(-\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}+\beta_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

(145)

where
L_{{\bf{y}}}=\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}
is defined as in (
44
) and
L_{\mu_{g}}=\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
.

Proof.

From Lemma
B.4
, we have for any
c>0

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})+\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+c\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{c}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(146)

From Lemma
D.8
, we have for any
a>0

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+a\right)\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(-(1+a)\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{a}\right)\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}\right].

(147)

Substituting (
D.3
) into (
D.3
), we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+c)(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(-(1+c)(1+a)\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(1+c)(1+\frac{1}{a})\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}\right]+\left(1+\frac{1}{c}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(148)

Choose
c=\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}/2}{1-\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}
and
a=\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{1-2\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}
. Then, the following equations and inequalities are satisfied.

\displaystyle(1+c)(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2},

(149)

\displaystyle(1+a)\left(1-2\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}},

\displaystyle(1+c)\left(1-\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2},

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{a}\leq\frac{1}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}},\quad 1+\frac{1}{c}\leq\frac{2}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}},

where
L_{\mu_{g}}=\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
. Based on (
D.3
) and (
149
), we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\left(-\left(\frac{2\beta_{t}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\beta_{t}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}\beta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\beta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

(150)

Next, we upper-bound the last term of the above inequality.

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\left({\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}+\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\right),

(151)

where the second inequality is by Lemma
D.2
.

Moreover, from Lemma
D.6
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{6\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}.

(152)

Combining (
D.3
) and (
D.3
) yields

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}\right]+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{6\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}\right).

(153)

Substituting (
D.3
) into (
D.3
) and summing over
t\in[T]
, give the desired result.

∎

D.4
Bounds on the Zeroth-Order System Solution

Lemma D.10
.

Suppose Assumptions
B2.
and
B3.
hold.
Let

\vartheta:={\mathbb{E}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2},

where
\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}
and
\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}
are defined in (
25a
) and (
26a
), respectively.
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\vartheta

\displaystyle\leq(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{2}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}.

Proof.

From Lemma
D.5
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3d_{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\|{\bf{z}}_{t+1}-{\bf{z}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6d_{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6d_{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}.

(154)

Moreover, from (
26a
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3}{8\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{9}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{4}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{8\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2},

(155)

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
D.5
.

From
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\left(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}\right)
, we get

\displaystyle\vartheta

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{2}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from (
D.4
) and (
D.4
).

∎

Lemma D.11
.

Suppose Assumptions
B2.
,
B3.
,
D1.
, and
D3.
hold.
Consider the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm

2
, and define

\displaystyle{e}_{t+1}^{M}:={\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}},\quad\textnormal{where}

(156)

\displaystyle\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}\left({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}\right)=\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad\quad\quad\quad-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1})).

(157)

Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t+1}^{M}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-{\lambda_{t+1}})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}+36{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(18d_{2}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{2}\right)\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+18d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

Proof.
