Lemma D.12
.

Suppose Assumption
B4.
holds.
Let

\displaystyle e^{H}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\tilde{\nabla}_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t},

(164a)

\displaystyle e^{J}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t},

(164b)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)=\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})),

\displaystyle\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)=\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})).

Then, for
\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t}\right)
presented to Algorithm
2
, we have

(a)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e^{H}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

(165a)

(b)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e^{J}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

(165b)

Proof.

For part (a)
:
From Lemma
D.1
, We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e^{H}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\tilde{\nabla}_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}p^{2},

(166)

where the last inequality follows from (
2
).

For part (b):

From Lemma
D.1
, We have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e^{J}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}p^{2},

(167)

where the last inequality follows from (
2
).
∎

Lemma D.13
.

Suppose Assumption
B4.
holds. Then, for the directions
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
and
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}
provided to Algorithm
2
, and

(a)

for
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}
defined in (
22b
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{M}\right\rVert^{2}\right]+2\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}=:B_{t},

(168a)

where
{e}_{t}^{M}=\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
is defined as in (
156
).

(b)

and for
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{x}}}
defined in (
22c
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{x}}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{L}\right\rVert^{2}\right]+2\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4},

(168b)

where

\displaystyle{e}_{t}^{L}:=\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}},

(168c)

with
\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)
is defined in (
174
).

Proof.

For part (a)
:
Let

\displaystyle\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)=\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})).

(169)

According to the definition of
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}
in (
22b
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{v}}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

(170a)

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\tilde{\nabla}_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(170b)

Next, we separately bound (
170a
) and (
170b
) on the RHS of the above inequality.

Bounding (
170a
)
. We have

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]:=2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{M}\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(171)

Bounding (
170b
)
. From Lemmas
D.1
and
D.12
, we have

\displaystyle\eqref{eq00}={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{H}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

(172)

Combining (
171
) and (
172
) yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{v}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{M}\right\rVert^{2}\right]+2\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

(173)

For part (b):

Let

\displaystyle\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)=\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})).

(174)

According to the definition of
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{x}}
in (
22c
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{x}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

(175a)

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right){\bf{v}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(175b)

Next, we separately bound (
175a
) and (
175b
) on the RHS of the above inequality.

Bounding (
175a
)
. We have

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]:=2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{L}\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(176)

Bounding (
175b
)
. From Lemmas
D.1
and
D.12
, we have

\displaystyle\eqref{4r}={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{J}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

(177)

Combining (
176
)–(
177
) yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{x}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{L}\right\rVert^{2}\right]+2\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

∎

Lemma D.14
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
,
B1.
,
B3.
and
B4.
hold. Set the step size
\delta_{t}
and the parameter
p
in (
2
), as

\displaystyle\delta_{t}\leq\left(2+\frac{1}{\ell_{g,1}^{2}}\right)\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}},\penalty\ \forall t\in[T],\penalty\ \quad\textnormal{and}\quad p=\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}.

(178)

Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
2
and
\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})
in (
20
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{a}\right)\left(1-\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\right)\delta_{t}^{2}B_{t},

for some
\acute{a}>0
, where

\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
and
B_{t}
are defined in Eq. (
144
) and Lemma
D.13
, respectively.

Proof.

By setting the radius
p:=\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}
in (
2
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\Pi}_{\mathcal{Z}_{p}}\left[{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\right]-{\Pi}_{\mathcal{Z}_{p}}\left[\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right]\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{a}\right)\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]}_{I_{t}}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\right)\delta_{t}^{2}\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]}_{K_{t}},

(179)

where
\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t}):=\nabla^{2}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right){\bf{v}}_{t}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))
.; the first inequality follows from non-expansiveness property of a projection operator.

We next bound the
I_{t}
, and
K_{t}
terms in (
D.4
), respectively.

Bounding
I_{t}

.
We have

\displaystyle I_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]-2\delta_{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle+\delta_{t}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle-\left(2\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}-\delta_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

where the inequality holds since
\nabla P_{t}
is the gradient of the strongly convex quadratic program
\frac{1}{2}{\bf{v}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\right){\bf{v}}+{\bf{v}}^{\top}\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}))
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\langle\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right].

Since
\delta_{t}\leq\left(2+\frac{1}{\ell_{g,1}^{2}}\right)\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
, then we have

\displaystyle I_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-2\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{\ell_{g,1}^{2}}\left(\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\delta_{t}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

(180)

where the second inequality holds since from (
20
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla P_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}),{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right){\bf{v}}_{t}+\nabla_{\bf{y}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla_{\bf{y}}^{2}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right)({\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right],

where the second inequality follows from Assumption
B3.
.

Bounding
K_{t}

.
Let

\displaystyle\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}\left({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}\right)=\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}\left({\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\right).

From Lemma
D.13
, we have

\displaystyle K_{t}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{\bf{v}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]\leq B_{t}.

(181)

Putting (
D.4
), and (
181
) together with Eq. (
D.4
) yields the desired result.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{a}\right)\left(1-\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right]+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\right)\delta_{t}^{2}B_{t}.

∎

Lemma D.15
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
and
2.3
hold.
Let
\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
be defined in (
144
). Set the parameter
p
in (
2
) as
p=\frac{\ell_{f,0}}{\mu_{g}}
.
Then, for any positive choice of step sizes satisfying

\delta_{t}\leq\left(2+\frac{1}{\ell_{g,1}^{2}}\right)\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}},

the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
2
guarantees the following bound:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(-\frac{L_{\mu_{g}}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]+\frac{4}{L_{\mu_{g}}}B_{t}\right)\delta_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{16\nu^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}}(2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}+1)\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\frac{96\ell_{g,1}\nu^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{3}}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})+\frac{48\nu^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}}\sup_{{\bf{x}}\in\mathcal{X}}\|{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t-1}({\bf{x}})-{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}})\|^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\delta_{t}},

(182)

where
B_{t}
,
\nu
and
(L_{\mu_{g}},L_{{\bf{y}}})
are defined in Lemmas

D.13
,
C.7
and
D.9
, respectively.

Proof.

From Lemma
B.4
, we have, for any
\acute{c}>0

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})+\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{c}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(183)

From Lemma
D.14
, we have, for any
\acute{a}>0

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{a}\right)\left(1-\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right)\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\right)\delta_{t}^{2}B_{t}.

(184)

Substituting (
184
) into (
D.4
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\acute{c}\right)\left(1+\acute{a}\right)\left(1-\delta_{t}\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}\right)\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\acute{c}\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\right)\delta_{t}^{2}B_{t}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(185)

Choose
\acute{c}=\frac{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}/4}{1-\frac{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{2}}
and
\acute{a}=\frac{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}/2}{1-\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}
.
Then, the following equations and inequalities are satisfied.

\displaystyle\left(1+\acute{c}\right)\left(1+\acute{a}\right)\left(1-\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}\right)=1-\frac{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{4},

(186)

\displaystyle(1+\acute{c})\left(1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\right)\leq\frac{4}{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}},

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{\acute{a}}\leq\frac{2}{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}},\quad 1+\frac{1}{\acute{c}}\leq\frac{4}{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}},

where
L_{\mu_{g}}=\frac{\mu_{g}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}}
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\delta_{t}L_{\mu_{g}}}{4}\right)\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}+\frac{4}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\delta_{t}B_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right].

(187)

We now bound the last term on the right-hand side of (
D.4
).
By Lemma
C.7
, we have:

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(2\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}+2\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t+1}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}+\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}\right),

(188)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.2
.

From (
D.3
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+3\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{6\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}.

(189)

Plugging (
D.4
) into (
D.4
), we get

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{v}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}(2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}+1)\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+4\frac{\nu^{2}}{\mu_{g}^{2}}\left(3\|{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{6\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}\right).

(190)

Then, substituting (
D.4
) into (
D.4
), rearranging the resulting inequality and summing over
t\in[T]
, we obtain the desired result.
∎

D.5
Bounds on the Zeroth-Order Estimation Error of Outer Objective

Lemma D.16
.

Suppose Assumptions
B2.
and
B3.
hold.
Let

\displaystyle\varpi:=\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2},

where
\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}
and
\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}
are defined in (
25b
) and (
26b
), respectively.
Then, for the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\varpi

\displaystyle\leq(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{2}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}.

Proof.

From Lemma
D.5
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{z}}_{t+1}-{\bf{z}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6d_{1}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6d_{1}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}.

(191)

Moreover, from (
26a
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3}{8\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{9}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{4}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{8\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2},

(192)

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
D.5
.

From
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\left(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}\right)
, we get

\displaystyle\varpi

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{9}{2}d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2},

where the second inequality follows from (
D.5
) and (
D.5
).

∎

Lemma D.17
.

Suppose Assumptions
B2.
,
B3.
,
D2.
, and
D4.
hold.
Consider the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
generated by Algorithm

2
.
For
{e}_{t}^{L}
defined in (
168c
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t+1}^{L}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{L}\|^{2}+36{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(18d_{1}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{1}\right)\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+18d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+27d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+3(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}}{{b}}){\eta}^{2}_{t+1}.

(193)

Proof.

According to the definition of
\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}
in Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}

\displaystyle=-\eta_{t+1}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}+\eta_{t+1}(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle+(1-\eta_{t+1})\left(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-(\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}})\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}+\eta_{t+1}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}

\displaystyle-\eta_{t+1}(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle-(1-\eta_{t+1})\left(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\|(1-\eta_{t+1})(\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}})

\displaystyle+\eta_{t+1}(\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle+(1-\eta_{t+1})\left(\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right)\|^{2}.

Since

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right]=\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1}),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right]

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t}),

then, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\eta_{t+1}(\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))

\displaystyle+(1-\eta_{t+1})\left(\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\right)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})

\displaystyle+\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})

\displaystyle-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\eta^{2}_{t+1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\eta^{2}_{t+1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2},

(194)

where the second inequality holds by
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

Note that for the last term on the right-hand side of (
D.5
), using (
174
) and (
26b
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1})-\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\|\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}(\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t+1}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}},

where the last inequality follows from Assumption
D2.
.

Then, from
{\mathbb{E}}\|a-{\mathbb{E}}[a]\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}\|a\|^{2}-\|{\mathbb{E}}[a]\|^{2}
and Assumption
D4.
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})

\displaystyle+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{t+1})+\hat{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t};\bar{\mathcal{B}}_{t+1})\|^{2}+3\eta^{2}_{t+1}(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}}).

(195)

Then, from Young’s inequality and Lemma
D.16
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+12(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+27d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+3\eta^{2}_{t+1}(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}}).

(196)

For the third term on the right-hand side of (
D.5
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

(197a)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(197b)

For (
197a
), we get

\displaystyle\quad\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2},

where the last inequality follows from Eq. (
D.2
).

Similary, for (
197b
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|+\frac{3\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2}.

Substituting these inequalities in (
197
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}.

(198)

For the second term on the right-hand side of (
D.5
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}d_{1}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{2},

(199)

where the last inequality follows from Eq. (
134
).

From (
D.5
), (
D.5
) and (
D.5
), we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t+1,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t+1}({\bf{z}}_{t+1})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t+1}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\eta_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\bf{x}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{x}}{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+36{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}f_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+18\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}d_{1}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{18}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{x}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{18\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+27d_{1}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{1}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+3\eta^{2}_{t+1}(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}^{2}}{{b}}).

∎

D.6
Bounds on the Zeroth-Order Objective Function and its Projected Gradients

Lemma D.18
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
,
B2.
,
B3.
, and
2.4
hold. Then, for the sequence of functions
\{f_{t,\bm{\rho}}\}_{t=1}^{T}
defined in Eq. (
18
), we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2M+V_{T}+\ell_{f,1}\left(1+2\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}\right)T\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\right).

Here,
V_{T}
is defined in (
11
); and
M
is defined in Assumption
2.4
.

Proof.

Note that, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)

(200)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

(201)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right).

(202)

From (
128
), we have

\displaystyle\eqref{40}\leq T\frac{\ell_{f,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{2},

(203)

and

\displaystyle\eqref{60}\leq T\frac{\ell_{f,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{2}.

(204)

Moreover, from Lemma
D.6
, we have

(
201
)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{f,1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert+\ell_{f,1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1})\right\rVert

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2T\ell_{f,1}\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right).

(205)

For the last term of the above inequality, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle=f_{1}({\bf{x}}_{1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{1}({\bf{x}}_{1}))-f_{T}({\bf{x}}_{T+1},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{T}({\bf{x}}_{T+1}))

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=2}^{T}\left(f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t-1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t-1}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2M+V_{T},

which implies that

(
201
)

\displaystyle\leq 2T\ell_{f,1}\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}+2M+V_{T}.

(206)

From (
203
), (
204
), and (
206
), we get the desired result.
∎

Lemma D.19
.

Suppose that Assumptions
2.2
and
2.3
hold. Let
f_{t,\bm{\rho}}
be defined as in (
18
).
Then, for

\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}
generated by Algorithm
2
,
for all
t\in[T]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{L}\right\rVert^{2}\right]+4\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}

\displaystyle+2M_{f}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]+{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right):=A_{t},

(207)

where
{e}_{t}^{L}
is defined in Lemma
D.13
, and

\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
,
\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}
are as defined in (
144
). Additionally,
M_{f}
is given in Lemma
D.2
.

Proof.

From
\|a+b\|^{2}\leq 2\left(\|a\|^{2}+\|b\|^{2}\right)
, we get

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{x}}}\right\rVert^{2}\right]

(208a)

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right],

(208b)

where
{\bf{d}}_{t,\bm{\rho}}^{{\bf{x}}}
is defined in (
22c
).
From Lemma
D.13
, we have

(
208a
)

\displaystyle\leq 4{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert e_{t}^{L}\right\rVert^{2}\right]+4\ell_{g,2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{4}.

(209)

Moreover, from Eq. (
124a
), we get

(
208b
)

\displaystyle\leq 2M_{f}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]+{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right).

(210)

Substituting (
209
) and (
210
) into (
208
), we conclude the desired result.
∎

Lemma D.20
.

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
,
2.3
, and
2.4
hold. Let the sequence of functions
\{f_{t,\bm{\rho}}\}_{t=1}^{T}
be defined in (
18
), and let
\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}
be given in Definition
B.1
.
Then, for any positive choice of step sizes satisfying
\alpha_{t}\leq{1}/{4L_{f}}
, for all
t\in[T]
, Algorithm
2
guarantees the following bound:

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 12M+6V_{T}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(6\alpha_{t}-3L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)A_{t}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(6\ell_{f,1}(1+2\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}})+\frac{3\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2})\right)\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\right),

(211)

where
V_{T}
and
A_{t}
are respectively defined in Eq. (
11
) and Lemma
D.19
.

Proof.

Due to the
L_{f}
-smoothness of the function
f_{t}
by Eq. (
39c
) in Lemma
C.1
,
f_{t,\bm{\rho}}
is also
L_{f}
-smooth. Hence,

\displaystyle\quad f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rangle+\frac{L_{f}}{2}\left\lVert{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=-\alpha_{t}\left\langle\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle+\frac{L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}}{2}\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}.

(212)

For the first term on the R.H.S of Eq. (
D.6
), we have that

\displaystyle\quad-{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})),\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}},\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}},\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]+\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]+\frac{A_{t}}{2},

(213)

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
B.7
; the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.19
.

Plugging the bound (
D.6
) into (
D.6
), we have that

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}-\alpha_{t})}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]+\frac{\alpha_{t}A_{t}}{2},

which can be rearranged into

\displaystyle\quad(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left[f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right]+\alpha_{t}A_{t}.

(214)

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right],

where the second inequaliy follows from non-expansiveness of the projection
operator.

Then, from Lemma
D.19
and Assumption
B2.
, we have

\displaystyle\quad{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3A_{t}+3\ell_{f,1}{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\right\rVert^{2}\right]+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3A_{t}+3\ell_{f,1}\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}+3{\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{\bf{x}}\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right],

(215)

where the last inequality is by Lemma
D.6
.

Combining (
D.6
) and (
D.6
) and summing over
t=1
to
T
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 6\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t+1},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t+1}))\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{3\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)+3\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)A_{t}

\displaystyle\leq 12M+6V_{T}+6\ell_{f,1}\left(1+2\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}\right)T\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{3\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)+3\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(2{\alpha_{t}}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right)A_{t},

where the second inequality is due to Lemma
D.18
.
∎

Lemma D.21
.

Let the sequence
\{({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t},{\bf{v}}_{t})\}_{t=1}^{T}
be generated by Algorithm
2
.

(a)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}\leq 2\beta_{t}^{2}\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2},

where
e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}
is defined in (
140
).

(b)

Suppose Assumptions
2.2
,
B2.
and
B3.
hold. Then, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}\leq 4\alpha_{t}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{{t},\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}\alpha_{t}^{2}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}+2A_{t}\alpha_{t}^{2},

(216)

where
A_{t}
is defined in (
D.19
).

(c)

Suppose Assumptions
B1.
,
B2.
and
B3.
hold. Then, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{t}^{2}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}+3d_{2}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}

\displaystyle+(12\ell_{f,0}^{2}+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}p^{2})\delta_{t}^{2}+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\delta_{t}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}^{2}}}\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}},

where
{e}_{t}^{M}
and
\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}
are defined in (
156
) and (
144
), respectively.

Proof.

For part (a):

From Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\beta_{t}^{2}\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta_{t}^{2}\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\beta_{t}^{2}\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}+2\beta_{t}^{2}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}.

(217)

For part (b):

From the update rule in Algorithm
2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{t}^{2}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left\lVert\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{x}}}-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}+A_{t}\right),

(218)

where the first inequality is by
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
; the second inequality follows from non-expansiveness of the projection
operator; and the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.19
.

The first term in the above inequality can be bounded as

\displaystyle\quad\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\lVert\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))-\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\ell_{f,1}\|\hat{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})-{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t})\|^{2}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\ell_{f,1}\frac{\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}+2\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2},

(219)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.6
.

Based on (
D.6
) and (
D.6
), we get

\displaystyle\|{\bf{x}}_{t}-{\bf{x}}_{t+1}\|^{2}\leq 2\alpha_{t}^{2}\left(2\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{{\bf{y}}}^{*}_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})}{\mu_{g}}+A_{t}\right).

For part (c):

From the nonexpansiveness of projection, we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\Pi_{\mathcal{Z}_{p}}\big[{\bf{v}}_{t}-\delta_{t}\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\big]-\Pi_{\mathcal{Z}_{p}}\big[{\bf{v}}_{t}\big]\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{t}^{2}\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{t}^{2}\|\hat{{\bf{d}}}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})-\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})\|^{2}+2\delta_{t}^{2}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{z}}_{t})+\tilde{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}^{2}g_{t}({\bf{z}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\delta_{t}^{2}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\delta_{t}^{2}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})+\frac{1}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}}({\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{t}^{2}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}+6\delta_{t}^{2}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\delta_{t}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}^{2}}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{3\delta_{t}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}^{2}}}\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2},

(220)

where the second equality follows from (
156
).

From Assumption
B3.
, Lemma
B.3
and (
2
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\ell_{g,1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{2}+2\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}.

(221)

Similarly, we get

\displaystyle\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}-\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\ell_{g,1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}p^{2}+2\ell_{g,1}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}.

(222)

Moreover, from Eq. (
134
) and Assumption
B1.
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}+2\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{2}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{2}+2\|{\nabla}_{{\bf{y}}}f_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d_{2}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{2}+2\ell_{f,0}^{2}.

(223)

Substituting (
D.6
), (
222
) and (
D.6
), into (
D.6
), we get

\displaystyle\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{t}^{2}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}+3d_{2}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}\delta_{t}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}

\displaystyle+(12\ell_{f,0}^{2}+6\ell_{g,1}^{2}p^{2})\delta_{t}^{2}+\frac{6\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}^{2}}}\delta_{t}^{2}\|{\bf{y}}_{t}-\hat{\mathbf{y}}^{*}_{t}(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}.

∎

D.7
Proof of Theorem
3.2

Proof.

\displaystyle+12(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t-1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+9d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+24d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+24d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}(1-\gamma_{t+1})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}+2\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}\gamma_{t+1}^{2}.

(224)

\displaystyle+\left(18d_{1}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{1}\right)\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+18d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+27\ell_{g,1}^{2}d_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+3(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}}{{b}}){\eta}^{2}_{t+1}.

(225)

Since
(1-\lambda_{t+1})^{2}\leq 1-\lambda_{t+1}

\displaystyle+\left(18d_{2}^{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d^{2}_{2}\right)\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+18d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{36}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t+1}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})-\nabla_{{\bf{y}}}g_{t}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}+\rho_{{\bf{v}}}{\bf{v}}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{x}}_{t+1}-{\bf{x}}_{t}\|^{2}+6(12\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{9\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{2\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{y}}_{t+1}-{\bf{y}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+27d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\bf{v}}_{t+1}-{\bf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}+3(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}}{{b}}){\lambda}^{2}_{t+1}.

(226)

Combining the outcomes
.

Let

\displaystyle\Lambda

\displaystyle:=\Gamma\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t+1}^{{\bf{y}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]\right)

\displaystyle+\Upsilon\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t+1}^{{\bf{v}}}]-{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]\right)+\frac{1}{\Phi}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t+1}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|e_{t}^{g_{\bm{\rho}}}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\Psi}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t+1}^{M}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{\Omega}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t+1}^{L}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{L}\|^{2}\right).

Here, we have

\begin{split}&\Gamma=\frac{11M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}c_{\beta}},\quad\Upsilon=\frac{52M_{f}^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}c_{\delta}},\\
&\Phi=\max\left\{240\frac{d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{L_{f}},\frac{12d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}}{L_{f}M_{f}^{2}}\right\},\\
&\Psi=\max\left\{720\frac{d_{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{L_{f}},27\frac{L_{\mu_{g}}}{\Upsilon L_{f}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d_{2}c_{\delta},\frac{144d_{2}\ell_{f,1}^{2}(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})c_{\beta}}{L_{f}\Gamma},\frac{36\ell_{f,1}^{2}d_{2}L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}}{L_{f}M_{f}^{2}}\right\},\\
&\Omega=\max\left\{720\frac{d_{1}\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{L_{f}},27\frac{L_{\mu_{g}}}{\Upsilon L_{f}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}d_{1}c_{\delta},\frac{144d_{1}\ell_{f,1}^{2}(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})c_{\beta}}{L_{f}\Gamma},\frac{36\ell_{f,1}^{2}d_{1}L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}}{L_{f}M_{f}^{2}}\right\},\end{split}

(227)

with

(228)

\displaystyle c_{\beta}\geq\sqrt{1760}\frac{L_{{\bf{y}}}M_{f}}{L_{\mu_{g}}},

\displaystyle c_{\delta}\geq\sqrt{33280(1+2L_{{\bf{y}}}^{2})}\frac{\nu M_{f}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}},

\displaystyle c\geq\left(\max\left\{4L_{f},c_{\beta}(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1}),\frac{48L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}d_{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2}}{M_{f}^{2}\Phi}\right\}\right)^{3}+1,

\displaystyle c_{{\bf{v}}}=\max\left\{1080\ell_{g,1}^{2},\frac{324}{M_{f}^{2}}\ell_{g,1}^{4}c_{\delta}^{2},\frac{54L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}}{M_{f}^{2}}\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}^{2},\frac{216}{\Gamma}\ell_{g,1}^{2}c_{\beta}(\mu_{g}+\ell_{g,1})\right\}(\frac{d_{2}}{\Psi}+\frac{d_{1}}{\Omega}),

\displaystyle c_{\gamma}=\frac{26M_{f}^{2}\Phi}{L_{\mu_{g}}^{2}},

\displaystyle c_{\eta}=26\Omega,\quad c_{\lambda}=\frac{10\Upsilon}{L_{\mu_{g}}}c_{\delta}\Psi.

By adding (
D.7
), (

), (
D.7
), (

), along with (

) and considering the fact that

decreases with respect to

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{t=1}^{T}A(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t},\delta_{t},\rho_{{\bf{v}}}){\mathbb{E}}\left[\left\lVert\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X},\alpha_{t}}\left({\bf{x}}_{t};\nabla f_{t,\bm{\rho}}({\bf{x}}_{t},{\bf{y}}_{t}^{*}({\bf{x}}_{t}))\right)\right\rVert^{2}\right]+\Lambda

\displaystyle\leq 12M+6V_{T}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}B(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t},\delta_{t},\rho_{{\bf{v}}}){\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{v}}}]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}C(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t},\delta_{t},\rho_{{\bf{v}}}){\mathbb{E}}[\hat{\theta}_{t}^{{\bf{y}}}]

(229a)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E(\beta_{t},\delta_{t},\rho_{{\bf{v}}})\alpha^{2}_{t}(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})+\sum_{t=1}^{T}L(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t},\delta_{t},\rho_{{\bf{v}}}){\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{L}\|^{2}

(229b)

\displaystyle+\frac{8\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{4}\Upsilon}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\delta_{t}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}+4\ell_{g,2}^{2}p^{4}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(6\alpha_{t}-3L_{f}\alpha^{2}_{t}+2\alpha^{2}_{t}E(\beta_{t},\delta_{t},\rho_{{\bf{v}}})\right)\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{12}{L_{\mu_{g}}}\frac{\Gamma}{\beta_{T}}+\frac{48\nu^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{2}}\frac{\Upsilon}{\delta_{T}}\right)H_{2,T}+\sum_{t=1}^{T}M(\delta_{t}){\mathbb{E}}\|{e}_{t}^{M}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{36}{\Psi}D_{{\bf{y}},T}+\frac{36}{\Omega}D_{{\bf{x}},T}+\frac{12}{\Phi}G_{{\bf{y}},T}+\frac{18}{\Psi\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}G_{{\bf{v}},T}+\frac{18}{\Omega\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}G_{{\bf{x}},T}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{c_{\gamma}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}}{\Phi}\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}+3(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}}{{b}})\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\frac{c_{\lambda}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}}{\Psi}+3(\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}}{{b}})\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{c_{\eta}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}}{\Omega}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\left(\frac{\hat{\sigma}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}^{2}}{\bar{b}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{y}}}}}{\bar{b}\alpha_{t}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{y}}}}}{{b}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{g_{{\bf{x}}}}}{\bar{b}\alpha_{t}}+\frac{\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{f_{{\bf{x}}}}}{{b}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\alpha_{t}^{2}\right).

(249)

Bounding (
229i
)
.
From
\beta_{t}=c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}
,
\delta_{t}=c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}
in (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle D(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t},\delta_{t})

\displaystyle=6\ell_{f,1}(1+2\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}})+\frac{3\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2})+\frac{24\ell_{g,1}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}}\frac{\Gamma}{\beta_{t}}+\frac{96\ell_{g,1}\nu^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{3}}\frac{\Upsilon}{\delta_{t}}

\displaystyle=6\ell_{f,1}(1+2\frac{\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}})+\frac{3\ell_{f,1}\ell_{g,1}}{\mu_{g}}(\alpha_{t}-L_{f}\alpha_{t}^{2})+\frac{24\ell_{g,1}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}}\frac{\Gamma}{c_{\beta}\alpha_{t}}+\frac{96\ell_{g,1}\nu^{2}}{L_{\mu_{g}}\mu_{g}^{3}}\frac{\Upsilon}{c_{\delta}\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha_{t}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}D(\alpha_{t},\beta_{t},\delta_{t})\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\alpha_{t}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}})\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\right)\right).

(250)

Moreover, we have

\displaystyle R(\rho_{{\bf{v}}})=9d_{2}^{2}\frac{\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{\Phi}+18d_{2}^{2}\frac{\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{\Psi}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})\frac{d^{2}_{2}}{\Psi}=\mathcal{O}\left((1+\frac{1}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{2}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\acute{R}(\rho_{{\bf{v}}})=18d_{1}^{2}\frac{\ell_{f,1}^{2}}{\Omega}+6(3\ell_{f,1}^{2}+\frac{3\ell_{g,1}^{2}}{4\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})\frac{d^{2}_{1}}{\Omega}=\mathcal{O}\left((1+\frac{1}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})d_{1}^{2}\right),

which, implies that

\displaystyle\quad R(\rho_{{\bf{v}}})T\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+\acute{R}(\rho_{{\bf{v}}})T\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+18d_{1}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{T\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}}{\Omega\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}+18d_{2}^{2}\ell_{g,1}^{2}\frac{T\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}}{\Psi\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left((1+\frac{1}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}})T(d_{1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+d_{2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2})+\frac{T}{\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}}(d_{2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+d_{1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2})\right).

(251)

From (
250
), (
D.7
) and
\rho_{{\bf{v}}}^{2}=c_{{\bf{v}}}\alpha_{t}
in (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\eqref{3}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\alpha_{t}+\frac{1}{\alpha_{t}})\left(\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2}+\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}\right)+(1+\frac{1}{\alpha_{T}})T(d_{2}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{r}}}^{2}+d_{1}^{2}\rho_{{\bf{s}}}^{2})\right).

(252)

Combining the outcomes (
229i
)
.
Combining inequalities (

), (

), (

), (

), (

), (
